                    "text": "     1\t     1\timport { sql } from \"drizzle-orm\";\n     2\t     2\timport {\n     3\t     3\t  boolean,\n     4\t     4\t  index,\n     5\t     5\t  integer,\n     6\t     6\t  jsonb,\n     7\t     7\t  pgTable,\n     8\t     8\t  text,\n     9\t     9\t  timestamp,\n    10\t    10\t  uniqueIndex,\n    11\t    11\t  uuid,\n    12\t    12\t} from \"drizzle-orm/pg-core\";\n    13\t    13\t\n    14\t    14\texport const skills = pgTable(\n    15\t    15\t  \"skills\",\n    16\t    16\t  {\n    17\t    17\t    id: uuid(\"id\").primaryKey().defaultRandom(),\n    18\t    18\t    orgId: text(\"org_id\").notNull().default(\"\"),\n    19\t    19\t    createdByUserId: text(\"created_by_user_id\").notNull(),\n    20\t    20\t    createdBy: text(\"created_by_user_name\"),\n    21\t    21\t    updatedByUserId: text(\"updated_by_user_id\"),\n    22\t    22\t    updatedBy: text(\"updated_by_user_name\"),\n    23\t    23\t    scope: text(\"scope\").notNull().default(\"private\"),\n    24\t    24\t    sharedWithTeamIds: uuid(\"shared_with_team_ids\").array(),\n    25\t    25\t    name: text(\"name\").notNull(),\n    26\t    26\t    slug: text(\"slug\").notNull(),\n    27\t    27\t    description: text(\"description\").notNull(),\n    28\t    28\t    instructions: text(\"instructions\").notNull(),\n    29\t    29\t    argumentHint: text(\"argument_hint\"),\n    30\t    30\t    userInvocable: boolean(\"user_invocable\").notNull().default(true),\n    31\t    31\t    disableModelInvocation: boolean(\"disable_model_invocation\")\n    32\t    32\t      .notNull()\n    33\t    33\t      .default(false),\n    34\t    34\t    version: integer(\"version\").notNull().default(1),\n    35\t    35\t    isBuiltin: boolean(\"is_builtin\").notNull().default(false),\n    36\t    36\t    // ── Multi-file support (Agent Skills standard) ─────────────────────────\n    37\t    37\t    /** Map of relative path → { content, type } for multi-file skills.\n    38\t    38\t     *  e.g. { \"SKILL.md\": { content: \"---\\nname:...\", type: \"file\" },\n    39\t    39\t     *         \"scripts/init.sh\": { content: \"#!/bin/bash...\", type: \"file\" } }\n    40\t    40\t     *  When present, the worker writes each file to the sandbox at\n    41\t    41\t     *  /skills/<slug>/<relativePath>. The instructions field is kept in sync\n    42\t    42\t     *  with files[\"SKILL.md\"].content for backward compatibility. */\n    43\t    43\t    files: jsonb(\"files\").$type<Record<string, { content: string; type: \"file\" }>>(),\n    44\t    44\t    /** Space-separated tool restrictions (Agent Skills standard).\n    45\t    45\t     *  e.g. \"Read Grep ListDir\" — limits which tools the model may call while\n    46\t    46\t     *  the skill is active. */\n    47\t    47\t    allowedTools: text(\"allowed_tools\"),\n    48\t    48\t    /** Human-readable compatibility note (Agent Skills standard).\n    49\t    49\t     *  e.g. \"Requires Python 3.14+\" or \"Linux only\". */\n    50\t    50\t    compatibility: text(\"compatibility\"),\n    51\t    51\t    /** Activation triggers (Agent Skills standard).\n    52\t    52\t     *  e.g. [\"user\", \"model\"] — \"user\" means user can invoke; \"model\" means\n    53\t    53\t     *  the model may auto-activate the skill. Stored as JSON array. */\n    54\t    54\t    triggers: jsonb(\"triggers\").$type<string[]>(),\n    55\t    55\t    // ── Provenance tracking ─────────────────────────────────────────────────\n    56\t    56\t    /** URL this skill was imported from (GitHub directory URL, raw URL, etc). */\n    57\t    57\t    sourceUrl: text(\"source_url\"),\n    58\t    58\t    /** How this skill was created:\n    59\t    59\t     *  \"manual\" — written by hand via the form UI\n    60\t    60\t     *  \"ai-generated\" — created via POST /v1/ai/generate-skill\n    61\t    61\t     *  \"imported\" — imported from a GitHub/raw URL\n    62\t    62\t     *  \"builtin\" — installed from the built-in library catalog */\n    63\t    63\t    sourceType: text(\"source_type\").$type<\"manual\" | \"ai-generated\" | \"imported\" | \"builtin\">(),\n    64\t    64\t    createdAt: timestamp(\"created_at\", { withTimezone: true })\n    65\t    65\t      .notNull()\n    66\t    66\t      .defaultNow(),\n    67\t    67\t    updatedAt: timestamp(\"updated_at\", { withTimezone: true }),\n    68\t    68\t    deletedAt: timestamp(\"deleted_at\", { withTimezone: true }),\n    69\t    69\t  },\n    70\t    70\t  (table) => [\n    71\t    71\t    index(\"skills_org_id_idx\").on(table.orgId),\n    72\t    72\t    // Serves the list query pattern `WHERE org_id = $1 AND deleted_at IS NULL\n    73\t    73\t    // ... ORDER BY created_at LIMIT n`: Postgres can walk this index in\n    74\t    74\t    // created_at order and stop after LIMIT rows instead of scanning + sorting\n    75\t    75\t    // the whole org partition.\n    76\t    76\t    index(\"skills_org_deleted_created_idx\").on(\n    77\t    77\t      table.orgId,\n    78\t    78\t      table.deletedAt,\n    79\t    79\t      table.createdAt,\n    80\t    80\t    ),\n    81\t    81\t    uniqueIndex(\"skills_org_id_slug_uidx\")\n    82\t    82\t      .on(table.orgId, table.slug)\n    83\t    83\t      .where(sql`${table.deletedAt} IS NULL`),\n    84\t    84\t    uniqueIndex(\"skills_org_id_name_uidx\")\n    85\t    85\t      .on(table.orgId, table.name)\n    86\t    86\t      .where(sql`${table.deletedAt} IS NULL`),\n    87\t    87\t    index(\"skills_shared_with_team_ids_gin_idx\").using(\"gin\", table.sharedWithTeamIds),\n    88\t    88\t  ],\n    89\t    89\t);\n    90\t    90\t\n    91\t    91\texport type Skill = typeof skills.$inferSelect;\n    92\t    92\texport type NewSkill = typeof skills.$inferInsert;",
                    "type": "text"

            "id": "run-01a0d657-f766-719d-9425-2e44620ec4fb-tool-call-2b268c00-fab2-448a-b7af-334ac65da60e-5",

                {
                    "text": "     1\t     1\timport type { NewSkill, Skill } from \"@code/shared/db/schema\";\n     2\t     2\t\n     3\t     3\timport { ForbiddenError, NotFoundError, ValidationError } from \"../errors.js\";\n     4\t     4\timport { addBreadcrumb } from \"../telemetry/sentry.js\";\n     5\t     5\timport type { SkillFilters, SkillRepository } from \"../repositories/skill.repository.js\";\n     6\t     6\timport type { PagedResult, PaginationParams } from \"../types/pagination.js\";\n     7\t     7\timport type { RequestIdentity } from \"../types/identity.js\";\n     8\t     8\timport { canModify, type CallerIdentity } from \"../utils/resource-auth.js\";\n     9\t     9\t\n    10\t    10\texport type CreateSkillInput = Omit<\n    11\t    11\t  NewSkill,\n    12\t    12\t  | \"id\"\n    13\t    13\t  | \"orgId\"\n    14\t    14\t  | \"createdByUserId\"\n    15\t    15\t  | \"createdBy\"\n    16\t    16\t  | \"slug\"\n    17\t    17\t  | \"version\"\n    18\t    18\t  | \"createdAt\"\n    19\t    19\t  | \"updatedAt\"\n    20\t    20\t  | \"deletedAt\"\n    21\t    21\t>;\n    22\t    22\texport type UpdateSkillInput = Partial<\n    23\t    23\t  Pick<\n    24\t    24\t    NewSkill,\n    25\t    25\t    | \"name\"\n    26\t    26\t    | \"description\"\n    27\t    27\t    | \"instructions\"\n    28\t    28\t    | \"argumentHint\"\n    29\t    29\t    | \"userInvocable\"\n    30\t    30\t    | \"disableModelInvocation\"\n    31\t    31\t    | \"files\"\n    32\t    32\t    | \"allowedTools\"\n    33\t    33\t    | \"compatibility\"\n    34\t    34\t    | \"triggers\"\n    35\t    35\t    | \"scope\"\n    36\t    36\t    | \"sharedWithTeamIds\"\n    37\t    37\t  >\n    38\t    38\t>;\n    39\t    39\t\n    40\t    40\texport class SkillService {\n    41\t    41\t  constructor(private readonly skillRepo: SkillRepository) {}\n    42\t    42\t\n    43\t    43\t  async list(\n    44\t    44\t    orgId: string,\n    45\t    45\t    userId: string,\n    46\t    46\t    teamId: string | undefined,\n    47\t    47\t    filters: SkillFilters,\n    48\t    48\t    pagination: PaginationParams,\n    49\t    49\t  ): Promise<PagedResult<Skill>> {\n    50\t    50\t    addBreadcrumb({\n    51\t    51\t      category: \"service\",\n    52\t    52\t      message: \"SkillService.list\",\n    53\t    53\t      data: { orgId, pagination },\n    54\t    54\t      level: \"info\",\n    55\t    55\t    });\n    56\t    56\t    return this.skillRepo.findByOrgId(orgId, userId, teamId, filters, pagination);\n    57\t    57\t  }\n    58\t    58\t\n    59\t    59\t  async get(id: string, orgId: string, userId: string, teamId?: string): Promise<Skill> {\n    60\t    60\t    addBreadcrumb({\n    61\t    61\t      category: \"service\",\n    62\t    62\t      message: \"SkillService.get\",\n    63\t    63\t      data: { id, orgId },\n    64\t    64\t      level: \"info\",\n    65\t    65\t    });\n    66\t    66\t    const skill = await this.skillRepo.findById(id, orgId, userId, teamId);\n    67\t    67\t    if (!skill) throw new NotFoundError(\"Skill\", id);\n    68\t    68\t    return skill;\n    69\t    69\t  }\n    70\t    70\t\n    71\t    71\t  async create(\n    72\t    72\t    orgId: string,\n    73\t    73\t    userId: string,\n    74\t    74\t    userName: string,\n    75\t    75\t    input: CreateSkillInput,\n    76\t    76\t  ): Promise<Skill> {\n    77\t    77\t    if (\n    78\t    78\t      input.scope === \"team\" &&\n    79\t    79\t      (!input.sharedWithTeamIds || input.sharedWithTeamIds.length === 0)\n    80\t    80\t    ) {\n    81\t    81\t      throw new ValidationError(\"sharedWithTeamIds is required when scope is 'team'\");\n    82\t    82\t    }\n    83\t    83\t    // Clear team IDs when scope is not 'team' to prevent inconsistent state\n    84\t    84\t    if (input.scope !== \"team\") input.sharedWithTeamIds = undefined;\n    85\t    85\t    addBreadcrumb({\n    86\t    86\t      category: \"service\",\n    87\t    87\t      message: \"SkillService.create\",\n    88\t    88\t      data: { orgId, userId, name: (input as Record<string, unknown>)[\"name\"] },\n    89\t    89\t      level: \"info\",\n    90\t    90\t    });\n    91\t    91\t    const slug = input.name\n    92\t    92\t      .toLowerCase()\n    93\t    93\t      .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, \"-\")\n    94\t    94\t      .replace(/(^-|-$)/g, \"\");\n    95\t    95\t    return this.skillRepo.create({\n    96\t    96\t      ...input,\n    97\t    97\t      orgId,\n    98\t    98\t      createdByUserId: userId,\n    99\t    99\t      createdBy: userName,\n   100\t   100\t      slug,\n   101\t   101\t    });\n   102\t   102\t  }\n   103\t   103\t\n   104\t   104\t  async update(id: string, orgId: string, identity: CallerIdentity, userName: string, input: UpdateSkillInput): Promise<Skill> {\n   105\t   105\t    if (input.scope === \"team\" && (!input.sharedWithTeamIds || input.sharedWithTeamIds.length === 0)) {\n   106\t   106\t      throw new ValidationError(\"sharedWithTeamIds is required when scope is 'team'\");\n   107\t   107\t    }\n   108\t   108\t    // Clear stale team IDs when scope leaves 'team'\n   109\t   109\t    const normalised = { ...input };\n   110\t   110\t    if (input.scope !== undefined && input.scope !== \"team\" && input.sharedWithTeamIds === undefined) {\n   111\t   111\t      normalised.sharedWithTeamIds = null;\n   112\t   112\t    }\n   113\t   113\t\n   114\t   114\t    // 1. Fetch current row — needed for auth check and demotion guard.\n   115\t   115\t    const current = await this.skillRepo.findByIdForAuth(id, orgId);\n   116\t   116\t    if (!current) throw new NotFoundError(\"Skill\", id);\n   117\t   117\t\n   118\t   118\t    // 2. Authorization: creator, admin (org-scope), or team_lead (team-scope).\n   119\t   119\t    if (!canModify({ creatorId: current.createdByUserId, scope: current.scope, teamIds: current.sharedWithTeamIds ?? null }, identity)) {\n   120\t   120\t      throw new ForbiddenError(\"You don't have permission to edit this skill\");\n   121\t   121\t    }\n   122\t   122\t    addBreadcrumb({ category: \"service\", message: \"SkillService.update\", data: { id, orgId, input }, level: \"info\" });\n   123\t   123\t    const skill = await this.skillRepo.update(id, orgId, userName, normalised);\n   124\t   124\t    if (!skill) throw new NotFoundError(\"Skill\", id);\n   125\t   125\t    return skill;\n   126\t   126\t  }\n   127\t   127\t\n   128\t   128\t  async delete(id: string, orgId: string, identity: RequestIdentity): Promise<void> {\n   129\t   129\t    const current = await this.skillRepo.findByIdForAuth(id, orgId);\n   130\t   130\t    if (!current) throw new NotFoundError(\"Skill\", id);\n   131\t   131\t    if (!canModify({ creatorId: current.createdByUserId, scope: current.scope, teamIds: current.sharedWithTeamIds ?? null }, identity)) {\n   132\t   132\t      throw new ForbiddenError(\"You don't have permission to delete this skill\");\n   133\t   133\t    }\n   134\t   134\t    addBreadcrumb({ category: \"service\", message: \"SkillService.delete\", data: { id, orgId }, level: \"info\" });\n   135\t   135\t    const deleted = await this.skillRepo.softDelete(id, orgId, identity.userId, identity.userName);\n   136\t   136\t    if (!deleted) throw new NotFoundError(\"Skill\", id);\n   137\t   137\t  }\n   138\t   138\t\n   139\t   139\t  async updateVisibility(\n   140\t   140\t    id: string,\n   141\t   141\t    orgId: string,\n   142\t   142\t    userId: string,\n   143\t   143\t    userName: string,\n   144\t   144\t    scope: string,\n   145\t   145\t    sharedWithTeamIds: string[] | null,\n   146\t   146\t  ): Promise<Skill> {\n   147\t   147\t    if (scope === \"team\" && (!sharedWithTeamIds || sharedWithTeamIds.length === 0)) {\n   148\t   148\t      throw new ValidationError(\"sharedWithTeamIds is required when scope is 'team'\");\n   149\t   149\t    }\n   150\t   150\t    // Clear team IDs when scope is not 'team' to prevent inconsistent state\n   151\t   151\t    const normalizedTeamIds = scope === \"team\" ? sharedWithTeamIds : null;\n   152\t   152\t    addBreadcrumb({\n   153\t   153\t      category: \"service\",\n   154\t   154\t      message: \"SkillService.updateVisibility\",\n   155\t   155\t      data: { id, orgId, scope, sharedWithTeamIds: normalizedTeamIds },\n   156\t   156\t      level: \"info\",\n   157\t   157\t    });\n   158\t   158\t    const skill = await this.skillRepo.updateVisibility(\n   159\t   159\t      id,\n   160\t   160\t      orgId,\n   161\t   161\t      userId,\n   162\t   162\t      userName,\n   163\t   163\t      scope,\n   164\t   164\t      normalizedTeamIds,\n   165\t   165\t    );\n   166\t   166\t    if (!skill) throw new ForbiddenError(\"You don't have permission to edit this skill\");\n   167\t   167\t    return skill;\n   168\t   168\t  }\n   169\t   169\t}",
                    "type": "text"

                    "text": "     1\t     1\t/**\n     2\t     2\t * resource-auth.ts — Scope-aware modify permission check.\n     3\t     3\t *\n     4\t     4\t * Single source of truth for \"can this caller edit this resource?\".\n     5\t     5\t * Pure functions — zero DB dependency, fully unit-testable.\n     6\t     6\t *\n     7\t     7\t * Authorization matrix:\n     8\t     8\t *   scope=private  → creator only\n     9\t     9\t *   scope=team     → creator OR team_lead whose teamId ∈ resource.sharedWithTeamIds\n    10\t    10\t *   scope=org      → creator OR admin\n    11\t    11\t */\n    12\t    12\timport type { UserRole } from \"../types/identity.js\";\n    13\t    13\t\n    14\t    14\texport interface ResourceOwnership {\n    15\t    15\t  creatorId: string;\n    16\t    16\t  scope: string;\n    17\t    17\t  /** sharedWithTeamIds from the DB row — null/undefined when scope != \"team\". */\n    18\t    18\t  teamIds: string[] | null | undefined;\n    19\t    19\t}\n    20\t    20\t\n    21\t    21\texport interface CallerIdentity {\n    22\t    22\t  userId: string;\n    23\t    23\t  role: UserRole;\n    24\t    24\t  teamId?: string;\n    25\t    25\t}\n    26\t    26\t\n    27\t    27\t/**\n    28\t    28\t * Returns true when the caller is allowed to modify (update/delete) the resource.\n    29\t    29\t *\n    30\t    30\t * Rules:\n    31\t    31\t *   private  → creator only\n    32\t    32\t *   team     → creator OR team_lead whose teamId is in resource.teamIds\n    33\t    33\t *   org      → creator OR admin\n    34\t    34\t */\n    35\t    35\texport function canModify(resource: ResourceOwnership, caller: CallerIdentity): boolean {\n    36\t    36\t  if (resource.creatorId === caller.userId) return true;\n    37\t    37\t  if (resource.scope === \"org\" && caller.role === \"admin\") return true;\n    38\t    38\t  if (\n    39\t    39\t    resource.scope === \"team\" &&\n    40\t    40\t    caller.role === \"team_lead\" &&\n    41\t    41\t    caller.teamId !== undefined &&\n    42\t    42\t    (resource.teamIds ?? []).includes(caller.teamId)\n    43\t    43\t  ) {\n    44\t    44\t    return true;\n    45\t    45\t  }\n    46\t    46\t  return false;\n    47\t    47\t}\n    48\t    48\t\n    49\t    49\t/** Numeric rank for a scope string. Higher = broader visibility. */\n    50\t    50\texport function scopeRank(scope: string): number {\n    51\t    51\t  if (scope === \"team\") return 1;\n    52\t    52\t  if (scope === \"org\") return 2;\n    53\t    53\t  return 0; // \"private\"\n    54\t    54\t}\n    55\t    55\t\n    56\t    56\t/**\n    57\t    57\t * Returns true when moving from currentScope to newScope reduces visibility\n    58\t    58\t * (i.e. the resource would become accessible to fewer users).\n    59\t    59\t */\n    60\t    60\texport function isDemotion(currentScope: string, newScope: string): boolean {\n    61\t    61\t  return scopeRank(newScope) < scopeRank(currentScope);\n    62\t    62\t}",

                    "text": "     1\t     1\t/**\n     2\t     2\t * Skill Import Route — imports a skill from a GitHub directory, GitHub file, or raw URL.\n     3\t     3\t *\n     4\t     4\t * POST /v1/skills/import\n     5\t     5\t *   Parses the remote source and returns a preview (files, name, description).\n     6\t     6\t *   The client reviews the preview and then POSTs to /v1/skills to create.\n     7\t     7\t *\n     8\t     8\t * Security:\n     9\t     9\t *   - URL allowlist: github.com, raw.githubusercontent.com, api.github.com\n    10\t    10\t *   - Private IP / loopback blocking\n    11\t    11\t *   - 10-second fetch timeout\n    12\t    12\t *   - 1 MB per file, max 10 files\n    13\t    13\t */\n    14\t    14\t\n    15\t    15\timport axios from \"axios\";\n    16\t    16\timport type { FastifyInstance, FastifyReply, FastifyRequest } from \"fastify\";\n    17\t    17\timport { z } from \"zod\";\n    18\t    18\t\n    19\t    19\timport { env } from \"../config/env.js\";\n    20\t    20\timport gatewayAuthPlugin from \"../plugins/gateway-auth.plugin.js\";\n    21\t    21\t\n    22\t    22\t// ── Constants ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n    23\t    23\t\n    24\t    24\tconst ALLOWED_HOSTS = new Set([\"github.com\", \"raw.githubusercontent.com\", \"api.github.com\"]);\n    25\t    25\tconst FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS = 10_000;\n    26\t    26\tconst MAX_FILE_SIZE = 1_048_576; // 1 MB\n    27\t    27\tconst MAX_FILES = 10;\n    28\t    28\t\n    29\t    29\t// ── Schemas ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n    30\t    30\t\n    31\t    31\tconst ImportBody = z.object({\n    32\t    32\t  source: z.enum([\"github-directory\", \"github-file\", \"raw-url\"]),\n    33\t    33\t  url: z.string().url().max(2048),\n    34\t    34\t});\n    35\t    35\t\n    36\t    36\t// ── Helpers ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n    37\t    37\t\n    38\t    38\tfunction assertAllowedUrl(rawUrl: string): URL {\n    39\t    39\t  let parsed: URL;\n    40\t    40\t  try {\n    41\t    41\t    parsed = new URL(rawUrl);\n    42\t    42\t  } catch {\n    43\t    43\t    throw new Error(\"Invalid URL\");\n    44\t    44\t  }\n    45\t    45\t\n    46\t    46\t  const host = parsed.hostname.toLowerCase();\n    47\t    47\t  if (!ALLOWED_HOSTS.has(host)) {\n    48\t    48\t    throw new Error(\n    49\t    49\t      `URL host \"${host}\" is not allowed. Supported hosts: ${[...ALLOWED_HOSTS].join(\", \")}`,\n    50\t    50\t    );\n    51\t    51\t  }\n    52\t    52\t\n    53\t    53\t  // Block private/loopback IPs\n    54\t    54\t  if (/^(127\\.|10\\.|192\\.168\\.|172\\.(1[6-9]|2\\d|3[01])\\.|::1|localhost)/.test(host)) {\n    55\t    55\t    throw new Error(\"Private or loopback URLs are not permitted\");\n    56\t    56\t  }\n    57\t    57\t\n    58\t    58\t  return parsed;\n    59\t    59\t}\n    60\t    60\t\n    61\t    61\t/**\n    62\t    62\t * Parse a GitHub web URL (https://github.com/owner/repo/tree/branch/path)\n    63\t    63\t * into API-compatible parts.\n    64\t    64\t */\n    65\t    65\tfunction parseGitHubUrl(url: URL): {\n    66\t    66\t  owner: string;\n    67\t    67\t  repo: string;\n    68\t    68\t  branch: string;\n    69\t    69\t  path: string;\n    70\t    70\t} {\n    71\t    71\t  // Supports:\n    72\t    72\t  //   https://github.com/owner/repo/tree/branch/path/to/dir\n    73\t    73\t  //   https://github.com/owner/repo/blob/branch/path/to/file\n    74\t    74\t  const segments = url.pathname.split(\"/\").filter(Boolean);\n    75\t    75\t  if (segments.length < 2) throw new Error(\"Invalid GitHub URL — must include owner and repo\");\n    76\t    76\t  const owner = segments[0]!;\n    77\t    77\t  const repo = segments[1]!;\n    78\t    78\t  const refType = segments[2]; // \"tree\" or \"blob\"\n    79\t    79\t  const branch = refType === \"tree\" || refType === \"blob\" ? (segments[3] ?? \"main\") : \"main\";\n    80\t    80\t  const pathStart = refType === \"tree\" || refType === \"blob\" ? 4 : 2;\n    81\t    81\t  const path = segments.slice(pathStart).join(\"/\") || \"\";\n    82\t    82\t  return { owner, repo, branch, path };\n    83\t    83\t}\n    84\t    84\t\n    85\t    85\t/**\n    86\t    86\t * Convert a GitHub web URL to a raw.githubusercontent.com URL.\n    87\t    87\t */\n    88\t    88\tfunction toRawUrl(owner: string, repo: string, branch: string, filePath: string): string {\n    89\t    89\t  return `https://raw.githubusercontent.com/${owner}/${repo}/${branch}/${filePath}`;\n    90\t    90\t}\n    91\t    91\t\n    92\t    92\t/**\n    93\t    93\t * Parse YAML frontmatter from a SKILL.md string.\n    94\t    94\t * Returns { name, description } or empty strings if not found.\n    95\t    95\t */\n    96\t    96\tfunction parseFrontmatter(content: string): { name: string; description: string } {\n    97\t    97\t  const match = /^---\\s*\\n([\\s\\S]*?)\\n---/.exec(content);\n    98\t    98\t  if (!match) return { name: \"\", description: \"\" };\n    99\t    99\t  const fm = match[1] ?? \"\";\n   100\t   100\t  const name = (/^name:\\s*(.+)$/m.exec(fm) ?? [])[1]?.trim() ?? \"\";\n   101\t   101\t  const description = (/^description:\\s*(.+)$/m.exec(fm) ?? [])[1]?.trim() ?? \"\";\n   102\t   102\t  return { name, description };\n   103\t   103\t}\n   104\t   104\t\n   105\t   105\t/** Build GitHub API headers, injecting token when available. */\n   106\t   106\tfunction githubHeaders(extra: Record<string, string> = {}): Record<string, string> {\n   107\t   107\t  const h: Record<string, string> = {\n   108\t   108\t    \"User-Agent\": \"code-Agent-Server/1.0\",\n   109\t   109\t    Accept: \"application/vnd.github.v3+json\",\n   110\t   110\t    ...extra,\n   111\t   111\t  };\n   112\t   112\t  const token = env.GITHUB_TOKEN;\n   113\t   113\t  if (token) h[\"Authorization\"] = `Bearer ${token}`;\n   114\t   114\t  return h;\n   115\t   115\t}\n   116\t   116\t\n   117\t   117\tasync function fetchText(url: string): Promise<string> {\n   118\t   118\t  const resp = await axios.get<string>(url, {\n   119\t   119\t    timeout: FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS,\n   120\t   120\t    responseType: \"text\",\n   121\t   121\t    maxContentLength: MAX_FILE_SIZE,\n   122\t   122\t    headers: { \"User-Agent\": \"code-Agent-Server/1.0\" },\n   123\t   123\t  });\n   124\t   124\t  return resp.data;\n   125\t   125\t}\n   126\t   126\t\n   127\t   127\t// ── GitHub directory import ───────────────────────────────────────────────────\n   128\t   128\t\n   129\t   129\tinterface GitTreeEntry {\n   130\t   130\t  path: string;\n   131\t   131\t  type: \"blob\" | \"tree\" | \"commit\";\n   132\t   132\t  size?: number;\n   133\t   133\t  sha: string;\n   134\t   134\t}\n   135\t   135\t\n   136\t   136\tinterface                     "text": "   166\t   166\t   * avoiding heap lookups at scale.\n   167\t   167\t   *\n   168\t   168\t   * @param orgId Organization ID\n   169\t   169\t   * @param userId User ID (created_by_user_id)\n   170\t   170\t   * @returns Count of active (not soft-deleted) templates\n   171\t   171\t   */\n   172\t   172\t  async countByOrgAndUser(orgId: string, userId: string): Promise<number> {\n   173\t   173\t    const [result] = await this.db\n   174\t   174\t      .select({ count: count() })\n   175\t   175\t      .from(sandboxTemplates)\n   176\t   176\t      .where(\n   177\t   177\t        and(\n   178\t   178\t          eq(sandboxTemplates.orgId, orgId),\n   179\t   179\t          eq(sandboxTemplates.createdByUserId, userId),\n   180\t   180\t          isNull(sandboxTemplates.deletedAt),\n   181\t   181\t        ),\n   182\t   182\t      );\n   183\t   183\t    return result?.count ?? 0;\n   184\t   184\t  }\n   185\t   185\t\n   186\t   186\t  async create(data: NewSandboxTemplate): Promise<SandboxTemplate> {\n   187\t   187\t    try {\n   188\t   188\t      const [row] = await this.db.insert(sandboxTemplates).values(data).returning();\n   189\t   189\t      if (!row) throw new Error(\"Insert returned no rows\");\n   190\t   190\t      return row;\n   191\t   191\t    } catch (err) {\n   192\t   192\t      if (isUniqueViolation(err)) {\n   193\t   193\t        throw new ConflictError(`An environment named \"${data.name}\" already exists in this organization`);\n   194\t   194\t      }\n   195\t   195\t      throw err;",
                    "type": "text"
                }

                    "text": "     1\t     1\t/**\n     2\t     2\t * Skill Import Route — imports a skill from a GitHub directory, GitHub file, or raw URL.\n     3\t     3\t *\n     4\t     4\t * POST /v1/skills/import\n     5\t     5\t *   Parses the remote source and returns a preview (files, name, description).\n     6\t     6\t *   The client reviews the preview and then POSTs to /v1/skills to create.\n     7\t     7\t *\n     8\t     8\t * Security:\n     9\t     9\t *   - URL allowlist: github.com, raw.githubusercontent.com, api.github.com\n    10\t    10\t *   - Private IP / loopback blocking\n    11\t    11\t *   - 10-second fetch timeout\n    12\t    12\t *   - 1 MB per file, max 10 files\n    13\t    13\t */\n    14\t    14\t\n    15\t    15\timport axios from \"axios\";\n    16\t    16\timport type { FastifyInstance, FastifyReply, FastifyRequest } from \"fastify\";\n    17\t    17\timport { z } from \"zod\";\n    18\t    18\t\n    19\t    19\timport { env } from \"../config/env.js\";\n    20\t    20\timport gatewayAuthPlugin from \"../plugins/gateway-auth.plugin.js\";\n    21\t    21\t\n    22\t    22\t// ── Constants ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n    23\t    23\t\n    24\t    24\tconst ALLOWED_HOSTS = new Set([\"github.com\", \"raw.githubusercontent.com\", \"api.github.com\"]);\n    25\t    25\tconst FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS = 10_000;\n    26\t    26\tconst MAX_FILE_SIZE = 1_048_576; // 1 MB\n    27\t    27\tconst MAX_FILES = 10;\n    28\t    28\t\n    29\t    29\t// ── Schemas ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n    30\t    30\t\n    31\t    31\tconst ImportBody = z.object({\n    32\t    32\t  source: z.enum([\"github-directory\", \"github-file\", \"raw-url\"]),\n    33\t    33\t  url: z.string().url().max(2048),\n    34\t    34\t});\n    35\t    35\t\n    36\t    36\t// ── Helpers ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n    37\t    37\t\n    38\t    38\tfunction assertAllowedUrl(rawUrl: string): URL {\n    39\t    39\t  let parsed: URL;\n    40\t    40\t  try {\n    41\t    41\t    parsed = new URL(rawUrl);\n    42\t    42\t  } catch {\n    43\t    43\t    throw new Error(\"Invalid URL\");\n    44\t    44\t  }\n    45\t    45\t\n    46\t    46\t  const host = parsed.hostname.toLowerCase();\n    47\t    47\t  if (!ALLOWED_HOSTS.has(host)) {\n    48\t    48\t    throw new Error(\n    49\t    49\t      `URL host \"${host}\" is not allowed. (segments[3] ?? \"main\") : \"main\";\n    80\t    80\t  const pathStart = refType === \"tree\" || refType === \"blob\" ? 4 : 2;\n    81\t    81\t  const path = segments.slice(pathStart).join(\"/\") || \"\";\n    82\t    82\t  return { owner, repo, branch, path };\n    83\t    83\t}\n    84\t    84\t\n    85\t    85\t/**\n    86\t    86\t * Convert a GitHub web URL to a raw.githubusercontent.com URL.\n    87\t    87\t */\n    88\t    88\tfunction toRawUrl(owner: string, repo: string, branch: string, filePath: string): string {\n    89\t    89\t  return `https://raw.githubusercontent.com/${owner}/${repo}/${branch}/${filePath}`;\n    90\t    90\t}\n    91\t    91\t\n    92\t    92\t/**\n    93\t    93\t * Parse YAML frontmatter from a SKILL.md string.\n    94\t    94\t * Returns { name, description } or empty strings if not found.\n    95\t    95\t */\n    96\t    96\tfunction parseFrontmatter(content: string): { name: string; description: string } {\n    97\t    97\t  const match = /^---\\s*\\n([\\s\\S]*?)\\n---/.exec(content);\n    98\t    98\t  if (!match) return { name: \"\", description: \"\" };\n    99\t    99\t  const fm = match[1] ?? \"\";\n   100\t   100\t  const name = (/^name:\\s*(.+)$/m.exec(fm) ??                     "text": "   291\t   291\t   * @param role User's role (admin, team_lead, member, viewer)\n   292\t   292\t   * @returns Maximum count, or Infinity for unlimited\n   293\t   293\t   */\n   294\t   294\t  private getLimitForRole(role: UserRole): number {\n   295\t   295\t    if (role === \"admin\") {\n   296\t   296\t      return Infinity;\n   297\t   297\t    }\n   298\t   298\t    if (role === \"team_lead\") {\n   299\t   299\t      return env.SANDBOX_TEMPLATE_LIMIT_TEAM_LEAD;\n   300\t   300\t    }\n   301\t   301\t    // member, viewer\n   302\t   302\t    return env.SANDBOX_TEMPLATE_LIMIT_USER;\n   303\t   303\t  }\n   304\t   304\t}",
